# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-26)

## Corpus Check
- 4654 files · ~10,770,064 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 108 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 34, .css 16, .entitlements 10)

## Summary
- 64985 nodes · 187616 edges · 1287 communities (1161 shown, 126 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7752 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `2f069afc`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
